        <script>
        function createCookie(name, value, expires) {
            document.cookie = name + "=" + value + "; max-age=" + expires + "; path=/";
        }
        function checkCookie(name) {
            const nameEQ = name + "=";
            const ca = document.cookie.split(';');
            for (let i = 0; i < ca.length; i = i + 1) {
                let c = ca[i];
                while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') c = c.substring(1, c.length);
                if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
            }
            return null;
        }
        // Initialize consent mode
        window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
        function gtag() { dataLayer.push(arguments); }

        // Check if user has already made a consent choice
        const consentCookie = checkCookie('user_consent');

        if (consentCookie) {
            // User has previously consented - parse their choices
            const consent = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(consentCookie));
            gtag('consent', 'default', consent);
        } else {
            // First visit - set default to denied
            gtag('consent', 'default', {
                'ad_user_data': 'denied',
                'ad_personalization': 'denied',
                'ad_storage': 'denied',
                'analytics_storage': 'denied',
                'wait_for_update': 500
            });
        }
    </script>

<div class="text-block post-block link-underline ">
    <h2 class="editorH2">Premier League Betting Markets</h2>

<p>At BettingOdds.com football odds are our passion and we have all the major Premier League odds available, whether outright&nbsp;or match betting.</p>

<p>We highlight the weekly matches with the Premier League betting odds and links to our&nbsp;match previews which have&nbsp;Premier League <a href="/football/tips">football&nbsp;tips</a>.</p>

<p>With our unique<strong>&nbsp;Probability Percentage</strong>&nbsp;feature which shows you what chance of winning the bookies rate each selection.</p>

<ul>
	<li><a href="/football/premier-league/winner"><strong>Premier League Winner Odds</strong></a>:&nbsp;Betting on who you think will win the Premier League title as never been simpler. Browse through our bespoke odds comparison grids to view the latest odds from the best online bookmakers. Clicking on your chosen odds - the best prices are in bold - will send you through to the bookmaker website where you'll be able to sign up and place your bets.</li>
	<li><strong><a href="/football/premier-league/top-scorer">Premier League Golden Boot Odds</a>:</strong>&nbsp;The Premier League top goalscorer award has been won by a series of elite marksmen, including Erling Haaland, who claimed the Golden Boot in each of his last two seasons.</li>
	<li><strong><a href="/football/premier-league/relegation">Premier League Relegation Betting</a></strong>: The frenzied battle for survival is always a&nbsp;nerve-shredding affair and will no doubt go right down to the wire in 2025/26. When placing a bet we recommend analysing the fixtures, each squad's depth and experience, and any managerial changes that have taken place.</li>
	<li><strong><a href="/football/premier-league/top-four-finish">Premier League Top 4 Finish Betting</a>:</strong>&nbsp;The race for a Premier League Top 4 Finish is always an exciting affair and one that proves popular from a betting perspective. To put it simply, the four clubs who finish in the prized top-four places will qualify for the group stages of the Premier League.</li>
	<li><strong><a href="https://www.bettingodds.com/thesackrace/next-premier-league-manager-to-leave">Next Premier League Manager To Leave Odds</a></strong>:&nbsp;There are a host of other outright markets available to bet on in the Premier League, including the ever-popular next Premier League manager to leave.&nbsp;Check out our&nbsp;<a href="/thesackrace">Sack Race</a> for all the latest vacancies and odds.</li>
	<li><strong><a href="https://www.bettingodds.com/football/pfa-player-of-the-year">Premier League Player of the Year</a></strong>:&nbsp;The Premier League Player of the Year award is dished out to the most outstanding footballer of the season, with recent victors including Bruno Fernandes, Mo Salah, Kevin De Bruyne, and Virgil van Dijk.</li>
</ul>

</div><div class="button-link-block post-block">
    <a href="/thesackrace/next-premier-league-manager-to-leave"         class="read-more-link"><span>Next Premier League manager to be sacked odds</span></a>
</div>

<div class="text-block post-block link-underline ">
    <h2 class="editorH2"><strong>Premier League Match Betting Markets</strong></h2>

<p>The final result of a Premier League match is the summation of a myriad of contributing factors. You can bet on all of these factors before or during a match, including the following:</p>

<ul>
	<li><strong>Match Result &ndash; </strong>Football betting at its simplest: wager on whether the result will end in a home win, an away win, or a draw.</li>
	<li><strong>Both Teams to Score &ndash;</strong> If you&rsquo;re expecting goals from two evenly matched teams, betting on both teams to score in the game might be a decent punt.&nbsp;</li>
	<li><strong>Match Result &amp; Both Teams to Score &ndash;</strong> Simply combine the two above bets together for higher potential returns.</li>
	<li><strong>Correct Score &ndash; </strong>One of the hardest bets to predict in the Premier League: wager on the precise full-time result.&nbsp;</li>
	<li><strong>Anytime Goalscorer &ndash;</strong> Select a player from either side to score at any time in the match.&nbsp;</li>
	<li><strong>First Goalscorer &ndash; </strong>Here you&rsquo;re picking the player to score the first goal in the match.&nbsp;</li>
	<li><strong>Player to be Carded &ndash; </strong>Select a player from either side to receive a yellow card.&nbsp;</li>
	<li><strong>Player Shots/Shots on Target &ndash;</strong> How many shots will Martin Odegaard take in Arsenal&rsquo;s opening game against Nottingham Forest? How many of those will be on target? Make your prediction in this betting market.&nbsp;</li>
	<li><strong>Over/Under Goals &ndash;</strong> In the over/under market, you&rsquo;ll make a prediction on how many goals will be scored in the match over or under a specified number.&nbsp;</li>
	<li><strong>Handicap &ndash;</strong> To level the playing field, bookmakers offer handicap bets on matches with seemingly foregone conclusions.</li>
	<li><strong>Bet Builder &ndash;</strong> One of the most popular betting types on Premier League games, the bet builder is a feature that allows punters to customise their own bet slip across a number of different betting markets. In theory, you could construct a bet builder across up to eight of the markets listed here.</li>
</ul>
